通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果
2026 年 10 月 15 日
一、 市場動態
1. 科技股領跌，費半連 6 日收黑：美股主要指數 10 月 14 日全數下跌，道瓊工業指數下
跌 0.09%，收 51,621.48 點；S&P 500 指數下跌 1.11%，收 7,718.94 點，為 7 月 29
日以來最大單日跌幅；Nasdaq 綜合指數下跌 1.88%，收 26,692.87 點，為 7 月 23
日以來最大單日跌幅。費城半導體指數下跌 4.30%，收 11,684.38 點，連續第６個交
易日走低，為 2025 年 5 月以來最長連跌紀錄。

2. 類股非全面下挫，資金仍呈輪動現象：半導體及 AI 相關股票在 10/14 跌幅尤其明顯
，AMD 下跌 5.73%、Nvidia 下跌 3.16%、Broadcom 下跌 3.10%，AI 基礎建設相關
的 CoreWeave、Nebius、Oracle 及 Dell 跌幅更深，顯示市場對資本支出規模較大、
融資成本敏感度較高的 AI 相關企業風險偏好明顯下降。不過，S&P 500 的 11 大類
股中有 8 類下跌，資訊科技及通訊服務為主要拖累來源，但醫療保健、必需消費與能
源類股仍逆勢上漲。
3. 美國 10 年期公債殖利率盤中急升後回落：美國 10 年期公債殖利率 10/14 由 5.2%左
右快速升至約 5.33%，逼近近期高點；然而，隨後市場消化 Fed 官員較審慎的政策訊
號，殖利率自高檔回落，終場約收於 5.19%，較前一交易日 5.21%小幅下降約 2 個基
點。
二、 下跌原因分析
1. 美 9 月通膨高於預期 ，Fed 再升息風險重新升高 ：美國 10/14 公布 9 月 CPI 月增 0.6%

、年增率也由 8 月的 3.4%上揚至 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%、年增率由 8 月的 2.4%
升至 2.6%。其中汽油價格單月大漲 9.1%，貢獻整體 CPI 月增幅超過一半；住宅成本
亦月增 0.4%，顯示物價壓力仍具黏性。此數據公布後，市場重新提高 Fed 進一步升
息的預期 ，CME FedWatch 所反映的 10 月升息 1 碼機率一度由前一日約 22%提高至
37%。雖然截至 10/15 台北時間早上 7 點左右該機率回降至 29.3%，這也提醒投資人
Fed 的升息路徑仍存在不確定性。
2. 高利率同時壓縮科技股估值與提高 AI 融資成本 ：科技及半導體公司未來獲利成長占評
價的比重較高，因此對折現率變化相對敏感。當長天期美債殖利率維持 5%以上，市
場願意支付的本益比自然受到限制，即使企業獲利仍維持成長，股價也可能因評價倍
數下降而出現修正。另一方面，本輪 AI 投資需要大量資料中心、伺服器、電力與網路
基礎設施，高利率亦直接增加企業融資成本，反映信用市場對高度依賴外部資金的 AI

基礎建設公司要求更高風險溢酬。

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. AI 投資焦點由「支出規模」轉向「投資報酬率」:近期市場對 AI 的疑慮逐漸由「需求
是否成長」 ，轉向「龐大的資本支出能否轉化為足夠營收、獲利與自由現金流」 。
Broadcom 執行長陳福陽近期亦指出 ，AI 未來發展的重要限制可能逐漸由晶片或記憶
體供應轉向資本本身。我們認為，這並不代表AI 資本支出週期即將結束，而是市場已
由第一階段的 「資本支出愈大、成長想像愈高」 ，逐步進入更重視資本效率與投資報酬
率的第二階段。未來財務結構、現金流與AI 商業化能力，將成為不同企業評價分化的
重要因素。
4. 中國記憶體競爭疑慮加重半導體短線壓力：中國長鑫存儲（CXMT）10/10 傳出記憶

體技術取得進展 ， 引發市場對未來DRAM 供給增加及價格競爭的疑慮 ， 記憶體相關類
股率先承壓，並進一步影響整體半導體市場情緒。惟 DRAM 與 GPU、客製化 ASIC、
先進晶圓製程及先進封裝的產業競爭與需求結構並不相同。我們認為，儘管記憶體競
爭升溫值得關注，但目前尚不足以延伸為整體 AI 半導體需求同步轉弱。
5. 高檔籌碼加速獲利了結：AI 與半導體仍是 2026 年美股表現最強勢的投資主題之一，
部分科技股今年累計漲幅已相當可觀。當通膨、殖利率及AI 投資報酬疑慮同時升高，
先前漲幅較大、投資人部位較集中的族群自然成為優先調節對象。因此，本波費半快
速修正除了總經與基本面疑慮外，亦包含籌碼降溫及風險部位收縮因素，使短期價格
波動幅度可能高於企業基本面實際變化。
三、市場看法
1. 類股輪動明顯，尚非全面看空美股：科技與 AI 相關股票 10 月 14 日明顯承壓，但醫
療保健、必需消費與能源類股仍逆勢上漲，道瓊工業指數僅下跌 0.09%。此外，S&P
500 成分股下跌家數與上漲家數之比約為 1.1：1；相較於指數下跌 1.11%，市場廣度

並未同步大幅惡化。這反映資金主要由高評價且前期漲幅較大的科技與AI 族群，轉向
現金流較穩定的防禦型產業。現階段市場特徵較接近類股輪動與風格切換，而非無差
別的全面風險撤離，亦尚未看到美股企業獲利或經濟基本面全面反轉的明確訊號。
2. 美股科技股財報前風險降載，Q3 仍存在重新定價空間：近期科技股修正，費城半導
體指數連續６個交易日下跌，也可能部分反映第三季財報季正式展開前，投資人在股
價先前漲幅較大、評價偏高及利率環境不確定下，提前降低風險曝險。真正決定後續
市場方向的關鍵 ， 仍在於實際企業獲利能否支持目前的高成長預期 。根據 Bloomberg
季度獲利成長追蹤資料，今年 Q1 與 Q2 財報季期間的 EPS 成長率經驗顯示，隨實際
財報陸續公布，企業獲利成長曾明顯優於財報季開始前的市場預期。儘管這並不保證
Q3 將複製前兩季的上修幅度，但目前市場因財報不確定性所反映的風險折價仍存在
被實際財報重新驗證的可能。若大型科技及半導體公司後續營收、獲利與AI 需求指引
實際公布結果優於預期，近期風險溢價可望部分收斂；反之，若獲利成長無法跟上目

前評價，市場整理時間則可能進一步延長。

不另行通知。
3. 費半修正幅度大，但 AI 需求尚未出現全面反轉：ASML 在 10/14 所公布的最新第三
季財報反而顯示 AI 相關設備需求仍相當強勁，該公司表示 2027 與 2028 年規劃產能
可能仍無法完全滿足客戶需求，部分客戶亦已表達對 2029 及 2030 年設備需求的興
趣。儘管費城半導體指數近期市場情緒轉弱，但由 ASML 的財報表現來看，目前仍未
看到大型 AI 客戶全面取消晶片訂單、主要雲端服務商同步削減 AI 資本支出，或先進
製程需求快速降溫等結構性訊號。
4. 台積電營收顯示 AI 需求暢旺 ， 留意10/15 法說會內容：台積電 10/8 公布的第三季營

收達新台幣 1.49 兆元、年增約 50%，創歷史新高，並高於市場原先預估的 1.46 兆元
；9 月單月營收年增 54.6%。強勁的營收表現仍反映 AI 與先進製程需求具有支撐。目
前市場因此呈現「企業營收仍快速成長，但股票評價下降」的狀況。高殖利率環境下
，EPS 上升與本益比下降可以同時發生，後續股價方向將取決於企業獲利上修幅度是
否足以抵銷評價收縮。該公司將於 10/15 下午舉行第三季法人說明會。市場重點將集
中於第四季展望、AI 與 HPC 需求、先進製程、先進封裝及未來資本支出規劃。公司
對 AI 需求與產能投資的最新判斷，將成為判斷近期費半修正主要屬於評價調整，或產
業基本面是否開始出現變化的重要依據。
5. AI 與半導體進入基本面分化階段：我們認為，在高利率環境下，高度仰賴外部融資、
自由現金流尚未成熟的 AI 基礎建設業者 ， 受到的資金成本壓力明顯高於具穩定現金流
的大型科技與雲端服務商。同樣地，記憶體、GPU、ASIC、先進製程及先進封裝各有
不同供需結構。我們預期下一階段 AI 行情將更加重視訂單能見度、產品競爭力、毛利

率、自由現金流及 AI 營收實際兌現，產業內部強弱分化程度可能進一步提高。
6. Fed 路徑仍存不確定性，惟企業獲利可望提供股市支撐：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使市場重
新評估 Fed 後續升息時點與幅度，美國利率政策仍存在不確定性，長天期美債殖利率
也可能維持高檔。不過，現階段企業獲利成長與AI 相關需求仍具韌性，若第三季財報

延續前兩季優於預期的趨勢，企業 EPS 若能上修有望部分抵銷高利率對本益比的壓縮
效果。換言之，後續市場表現未必只由 Fed 政策主導，企業實際獲利能否持續兌現，
將是支撐科技與半導體股中期走勢的重要力量。
四、投資建議
1. 短線聚焦美股財報與 Fed 決策，科技股中期趨勢料不致有變：近期市場同時面臨財報
前的觀望態勢、美債殖利率偏高及 AI 資本支出疑慮等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能
受到壓抑，後續宜關注 10 月美股財報對企業獲利與資本支出展望，以及 FOMC 會議
所釋出的政策訊號。中期而言，目前AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡

象，科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，但在利率維持高檔及市場評價偏高的環境下，後
續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出的實際變化。
2. 中天期債券風險報酬仍較均衡 ：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使短端殖利率持續受到 Fed 政策路
徑影響，而 20～30 年超長端仍面臨財政赤字、國債供給與期限溢價偏高等結構性壓
力。相較之下，目前殖利率已位於歷史相對高檔，若後續核心通膨或能源價格改善，
中段殖利率具有回落條件，同時其存續期間風險又低於超長端，因此現階段以中天期
美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

Document 055: 2026年9月物价数据点评：国际油价延续上行叠加芯片价格上涨，9月国内物价涨幅扩大

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年9月物价数据点评：国际油价延续上行叠加芯片价格上涨，9月国内物价涨幅扩大.pdf

### Page 1

东方金诚宏观研究

1 
国际油价延续上行叠加芯片价格上涨，9 月国内物价涨幅扩大 
——2026 年 9 月物价数据点评 
研究发展部执行总监 冯琳 
事件：根据国家统计局10 月14 日公布的数据，2026 年9 月，CPI 同比上涨0.9%，上月为上涨0.8%，
1-9 月 CPI 累计同比为 0.9%，2025 年 CPI 累计同比为 0.0%；9 月 PPI 同比上涨 5.0%，上月为上涨 3.8%，
1-9 月累计同比上涨2.3%，2025 年累计同比下降2.6%。 
基本观点：9 月中东局势升级，国际油价再度上涨，推动国内汽油价格上调，是当月 CPI 同比涨幅扩
大的主要原因。另外，受全球AI 投资热潮带动芯片价格大幅上冲影响，9 月国内电脑、手机等电子产品价
格继续上涨，对整体物价水平也有推升作用。不过，伴随促消费政策效应有所减弱，9 月汽车、家电等耐
用消费品价格走低，是限制当月 CPI 涨幅的一个主要因素。9 月剔除变动较大的能源和食品价格，更能反
映基本物价水平的核心 CPI 同比为 1.0%，继续处于偏低水平，背后主要是当前消费市场仍然呈现明显的供
强需弱特征。预计10 月CPI 同比涨幅会在1.0%左右，年底前会持续处于温和偏低状态。9 月PPI 环比涨势
加快，同比涨幅明显扩大，主要受国际输入性因素影响。当月国际原油、有色金属价格上行并向国内相关
产业传导，提振国内石油、有色金属产业链价格普遍上涨。同时，基建活动回暖叠加供给端限制带动国内
煤炭、钢铁等基础原材料价格回升，以及AI 投资热潮推动半导体产业链价格上涨等因素，对9 月PPI 涨势
也起到助推作用。预计10 月PPI 同比涨幅会扩大至5.2%左右，上行斜率明显放缓，后期有波动下行趋势。 
具体来看： 
一、9 月中东局势升级，国际油价再度上涨，推动国内汽油价格上调，是当月 CPI 同比涨幅扩大的主
要原因。另外，受全球AI 投资热潮带动芯片价格大幅上冲影响，9 月国内电脑、手机等电子产品价格继续
上涨，对整体物价水平也有推升作用。不过，伴随促消费政策效应有所减弱，9 月汽车、家电等耐用消费
品价格走低，是限制当月 CPI 涨幅的一个主要因素。9 月剔除变动较大的能源和食品价格，更能反映基本
物价水平的核心CPI 同比为1.0%，继续处于偏低水平，背后主要是当前消费市场仍然呈现明显的供强需弱
特征。 
9 月 CPI 环比上行0.2%，符合季节性规律，同比涨幅扩大至0.9%，较上月扩大0.1 个百分点。背后的
主要推动因素是在当月中东冲突升温、国际油价再度上涨背景下，国内汽油价格上涨4.9%，叠加上年同期

### Page 2

2 
价格基数下降，9 月汽油价格同比涨幅达到16.6%，比上月扩大7.3 个百分点；仅此一项就影响9 月CPI 同
比上涨 0.47 个百分点，影响幅度比上月扩大0.19 个百分点，是推动当月整体CPI 同比涨幅略有扩大的主
要原因。此外，受全球 AI 投资热潮带动芯片价格上涨影响，9 月国内手机、计算机、平板电脑、数据存储
设备等电子产品价格继续上涨，价格同比涨幅都有不同程度扩大，也对9 月整体 CPI 同比有一定推升作用。 
不过，当前以旧换新政策对汽车、家电等耐用消费品价格支撑作用减弱，9 月这两类补贴类耐用消费
品价格环比下行，叠加上年同期基数走高，价格同比分别下降1.7%和1.8%，降幅较上月扩大0.1 和0.9 个
百分点，是制约当月 CPI 同比涨幅的一个主要原因。其他方面，受美联储加息带动，9 月国际金价大幅下
跌，国内金饰品价格同步走低，价格同比涨幅从上月的33.6%回落到22.5%。 
食品价格方面，受中秋、国庆假期需求增加等影响，9 月猪肉、蔬菜、水果价格环比上行，猪肉价格
同比降幅收窄。不过，上年同期基数偏高影响下，当月蔬菜、水果价格同比降幅都有所扩大。整体上看，
9 月食品价格同比降幅较上月扩大 0.3 个百分点，达到-1.7%。值得注意的是，受供应仍然偏紧影响，9 月
鸡蛋价格环比高位上行，同比涨幅扩大0.7 个百分点至18.5%。 
服务价格方面，9 月暑期结束，旅游出行消费需求放缓，飞机票、宾馆住宿和旅行社收费价格都有季
节性下降，但受上年同期基数偏低影响，当月出行服务价格同比上涨 2.1%，涨幅较上月扩大 0.8 个百分点。
不过，由于教育服务价格同比涨幅回落，9 月服务价格同比涨幅与上月持平，为 0.8%。这也是 9 月扣除波
动较大的食品和能源价格、更能反映整体物价水平的核心CPI 同比涨幅为1.0%，与上月持平的主要原因。 
整体上看，中东局势屡现反复背景下，国际油价波动是当前牵动 CPI 走势的一个主要因素。值得注意
的是，全球AI 投资热潮对国内手机、电脑等商品价格的推升作用正在显现，但后期扩散至电子产品之外的
可能性不大。9 月核心 CPI 同比继续处在 1.0%的低位，显示当前物价涨幅偏低，主要受国内消费需求不振、
市场供强需弱拖累。 
往后看，10 月初国际油价处于高位，当月电子产品价格也会延续上行，我们预计10 月 CPI 环比会继
续上行，同比涨幅会升至1.0%左右，但仍将低于今年2.0%左右的控制目标。整体上看，年底前物价水平都
会处在温和偏弱状态。 
二、9 月 PPI 环比涨势加快，同比涨幅明显扩大，主要受国际输入性因素影响。当月国际原油、有色
金属价格上行并向国内相关产业传导，提振国内石油、有色金属产业链价格普遍上涨。同时，基建活动回
暖叠加供给端限制带动国内煤炭、钢铁等基础原材料价格回升，以及 AI 投资热潮推动半导体产业链价格
上涨等因素，对 9 月PPI 涨势也起到助推作用。

9 月 PPI 环比上涨1.2%，涨幅较上月大幅加快0.8 个百分点，带动当月同比涨幅从上月的3.8%扩大至
5.0%，创 2022 年下半年以来新高。可以看到，9 月制造业 PMI 中的主要原材料购进价格指数和出厂价格指
数分别达到 60.8%和54.0%，比上月上升4.2 个和3.6 个百分点，已预示当月PPI 涨幅会进一步扩大。9 月
PPI 涨势加速，主要受三个因素推动： 
一是 9 月中东局势升温，国际原油价格高位上行，国内成品油及化工产业链价格普遍出现较大幅度上
涨。其中，石油开采、精炼石油产品制造价格环比分别上涨14.5%和 7.4%，涨幅较上月加快 4.1 和 3.3 个
百分点，化学原料及化学制品制造、化学纤维制造价格环比分别上涨2.4%和2.1%，涨幅较上月加快2.5 和
1.7 个百分点。 
二是受稳增长政策发力、极端天气影响减退等因素影响，9 月基建活动增加，加之供给端限制，当月
国内主导的煤炭、钢铁、水泥等基础原材料价格全面上涨。其中，煤炭开采、黑色金属冶炼及压延加工、
非金属矿物制品行业价格环比分别上涨 9.5%、0.2%、0.3%，涨幅较上月分别加快 6.7、1.1 和 0.5 个百分
点。

及压延加工业价格环比分别上涨1.6%、1.5%，涨幅较上月分别加快2.5 和 0.7 个百分点。

7.7%（前值 2.7%），原材料工业价格上涨2.6%（前值 1.8%），加工工业价格上涨0.2%（前值 0.0%），显
示上游涨价势头明显强于中下游。同比来看，9 月生产资料价格同比涨幅较上月加快 1.5 个百分点至 6.5%，
分项中，采掘工业、原材料工业、加工工业价格分别上涨25.4%、9.4%和3.4%，涨幅较上月分别加快7.6、
2.7 和0.3 个百分点。

价格上涨 0.2%（前值 0.0%）；一般日用品价格环比止跌，从上月的下跌0.2%改善至 0.0%；衣着价格环比

缓。值得一提的是，近期耐用消费品价格上涨，可能受上游能源以及有色金属、半导体等原材料涨价带来
的成本压力驱动，这在近期手机等电子产品涨价趋势中得到体现，但汽车价格下跌对耐用消费品价格造成
一定拖累。同比来看，9 月生活资料价格同比下跌 0.1%，跌幅较上月收窄 0.4 个百分点。分项中，食品、
一般日用品价格同比跌幅收窄，衣着价格跌幅与上月持平，仅耐用消费品价格同比上涨，且涨幅在低基数

Technology - Asia-Pacific (H/A)

CCL for PCB: spec upgrades well on track 
toward CY27/28 with pricing upside

Price Objective Change

Accessible version

### Page 2

CCL for PCB: spec upgrades well on track
toward CY27/28 with pricing upside
Migration in raw materials of CCL
Spec advancements in resin more debatable
Major raw materials for building CCL (copper clad laminate) include cloth, resin, and
copper foil. Glass cloth serves as grounding and offers support to the CCL and PCB
(printed circuit board) structure, and it is immersed in resins built on different in-house
chemical recipes by CCL makers to obtain PP (prepreg). Afterwards, CCL makers

add/laminate the copper foil on this intermediate product (PP), to form the CCL supplied
to PCB customers. Across the previous generations (Exhibit 2), we have seen rising
penetration of PPE/PPO (polyphenylene ether/oxide) gradually replacing FR-4/ epoxy
resins throughout M4-M8 grade CCL, while hydrocarbon resin gradually gains traction
from M8-grade CCL. Yet, there is a debate on PTFE-based (polytetrafluoroethylene) CCL
vs hydrocarbon resin as the CCL grade advances into M9/M10.

As illustrated in Exhibit 4, we see a more diversified path of resin upgrades from M8-
grade CCL, while the advancement in copper foil and glass cloth seems to be more
certain. In general, we note that the mix of PPO vs hydrocarbon used is roughly 2:1 in
M8-grade CCL, and such proportion reverses to 1:2 in M9-grade CCL. Yet, the broader
range of resin options could potentially alter the competitive dynamics within the CCL
industry, since different vendors are promoting (or have edge) in different types of CCL

solutions. Or in other words, the likely penetration by PTFE-based solutions (pushed
mainly by NVIDIA) will favor the suppliers with experiences in handling relevant products
(though smaller market size such as base station), while, at the same time, it might
curtail the headroom for major Taiwanese suppliers, including EMC and TUC (Taiwan
Union), to grow.
PPO/BMI vs hydrocarbon in M8/M9; hydrocarbon vs PTFE in M9/M10
In our view, hydrocarbon resin exhibits an ultra-low dielectric constant (Dk) of 2.2-2.6

and a dissipation factor (Df) reaching 0.001-0.005, as low as 0.0005 at high frequencies
(e.g., 10 GHz and above), thanks to its non-polar molecular backbone. However, there are
drawbacks to hydrocarbon resin, including rather limited flexibility and high brittleness
after curing, which necessitate the mix with ductile polymers such as PPO or BMI
(bismaleimide) to improve the toughness (peel strength). Nonetheless, the performance
in Dk/Df would be reduced after adding PPO/BMI to hydrocarbon resin, which makes the

solution not that favorable in meeting customers’ demand/requirement.
Within hydrocarbon resin, we note there are two major advanced versions, including ODV
(oxidative vulcanization; lower-end) and BCB (benzocyclobutene; higher-end), and key
suppliers for the latter include Dow Chemical and Asahi Kasei. In terms of Df, <0.0008
can be reached by BCB type of hydrocarbon resin based on the standard of M9-grade
CCL, and the number would hit <0.0005 based on the standard of M9+/M10-grade. By

CCL suppliers, we note EMC (Elite Material) adopts hydrocarbon/BMI resin in M9-grade
CCL, while Panasonic blends PPO or BMI (with hydrocarbon) in M9 (vs PPO/hydrocarbon
in M7/M8). Shengyi Technology also adopts PPO resin in M9-grade offering.
Given the disadvantages (discussed above) of PPO resin, some CCL providers
alternatively also adopt mPPO (modified PPO; Exhibit 5) by blending the PPO with other
resins (e.g., epoxy methacrylate, styrene). By doing so, the overall dielectric and thermal

properties of the composite are enhanced, and the reduced molecular weight improves
solubility in solvents like toluene and MEK (methyl ethyl ketone). This in turn enables the
resin to thermoset, and it becomes more suitable for CCL processing. By application, we
believe it is adopted in the LPU (language processing unit) and UBB (universal
baseboard) under Vera Rubin platform, 1.6T networking switch, and will also be
potentially (competing with PTFE-based solutions) adopted in the backplane.

### Page 3

Similar to what we have seen in PPO/PPE vs hydrocarbon in M8/M9, we believe
hydrocarbon and PTFE will coexist and complement each other in M9/M10-grade CCL,
while the sheer adoption on one side is unlikely, given 1) different consideration on
cost/performance by different customers; and 2) the difficulty in sourcing across resin
and the adjacent equipment in producing different types of CCL (e.g. vacuum hot press
machine). Meanwhile, we note that PPO/PPE cannot be paired with PTFE given the

conflicts in characteristics.
In addition to the specialized plasma treatment, and long lead time in pressing machine
(operating under high temperature) with high costs, one of the other major drawbacks to
PTFE is the lack of stiffness in texture which makes it hard to be processed under PCB
drilling/lamination. Thus, SiO2 (silicon dioxide) is used as a filler (or with increasing
consumption vs that in M7/M8-grade CCL) to fill the resin matrix, and this helps reduce

the thermal expansion coefficient (CTE) and enhance the mechanical strength of the
composites. Elsewhere, a lack of adhesion to copper foil is the other key technological
challenge when processing PTFE-based solutions, and this requires the help from
hydrocarbon resin (to enhance the adhesion).
In short, the pursuit of more advanced materials like PTFE/hydrocarbon will
simultaneously incur other technological challenges, which, in our view, makes relevant

solutions to become more versatile (to solve the associated challenges). In Exhibit 6, we
present the promoted solutions of M9/M10-grade CCL in the market, and we compare
different metrics, including signal loss, mechanical strength, processibility, and costs.
Given the low penetration by M9-grade CCL currently (evidenced by single-digit% of
industry leader EMC’s revenue), we believe none of these solutions has become
mainstream. In other words, we deem the ongoing development and qualification/testing

results worth monitoring, and these include the feedback from board, ODM (original
design manufacturer) and end-customers’ levels. In our view, the most imminent (and
perhaps critical) one will likely be the solution adopted by Rubin Ultra switch board and
backplane (if any), which could be finally confirmed by end-2026/early-2027.

Exhibit 5: A comparison between PPO and mPPO 
mPPO carries enhanced processability and reactivity when compared with PPO 
Characteristics/type of 
resin PPO mPPO 
Dk Inherently low. Values for PPO-based composites are typically in the range of 2.1 - 3.5.  Retains low Dk, with high-performance grades achieving tightly controlled values, e.g., 2.8 - 3.0 for M9 grade. 
Df Inherently very low. Values for PPO-based systems can be as low as 0.0006. Significantly reduced and optimized. Ranges from 0.004 – 0.008 for M6/M7 grades down to <0.002 (typically 0.0015–0.002) for ultra-low loss M9 grades. 
Processability Limited. High melt viscosity and lack of reactivity make it difficult to process.  Better. Low molecular weight and reactive end-groups enable good solubility, compatibility with CCL manufacturing, and thermosetting cross-linking. 
Reactivity Low. Lacks sufficient reactive groups to cross-link effectively with other resins. High. Functionalized end-groups (e.g., styrene, methacrylate) allow it to chemically bond with other components in the resin formulation.

Clearer upgrades in glass cloth and copper foil  
While the upgrades in resin system appear to be more debatable with a versatility in 
solutions, we witness a firmer roadmap advancement in glass cloth and copper foil. For 
glass cloth, (see reports) Nittobo shared the view of 100% demand growth in NER this 
year (FY3/27), followed by another c60% growth next year. As for copper foil, Mitsui 
Kinzoku also revised up the mix of HVLP4 and above products in 1H26 (within very 
smooth profile copper foil) to 45% (was 40%), and the figure is expected to reach 60% 
in 2H26. On pricing, we note that Mitsui Kinzoku also mentioned it would try pursuing a 
higher ASP in product segments where demand remains strong, while there is a higher 
processing fee (Exhibit 7) charged by copper foil suppliers in the high-end copper foil 
market. All-in-all, we see positive indications to the high-end CCL pricing, given that the 
CCL makers will try to pass through the inflated costs (due to stronger demand and the 
premium in processing fee) to the product ASP.

Pricing gaps are significant across HVLP generations.

Source: BofA Global Research 
BofA GLOBAL RESEARCH

Ongoing spec migration on multiple fronts into CY27-28 
We see a clear upgrade cycle for CCL materials and PCB production technique across 
networking switches and AI (artificial intelligence) accelerators, including GPUs (graphic 
processing unit) and ASICs (application specific integrated circuits). In our view, the spec 
advancement (Exhibit 8) is primarily driven by faster data rates and increasingly 
stringent signal-integrity requirements. For NVIDIA, GB200/GB300 largely adopt M8-
grade CCL, with low-Dk1/2 glass cloth and HVLP2/3 copper foil. The transition to VR200 
drives further CCL specification upgrades toward M8+/M9-grade, along with the 
adoption of HVLP4 copper foil in VR200 compute board and midplane, while LPU adopts 
Q cloth with less signal loss (vs low Dk2).  
While NVIDIA tends to place greater emphasis on glass cloth upgrades, ASICs and 
networking switches are more focused on copper foil migration. Among ASICs, we 
expect the CCL spec for Google TPU (tensor processing unit) v8 to migrate to M8+

based on low Dk2 cloth and HVLP3 copper foil (vs M7-grade CCL with low Dk1 and
HVLP2 for TPU v7), followed by M9-grade adoption in TPU v9 based on HVLP4 copper
foil (with either low Dk2 or Q-cloth). As for the Trainium chips by Amazon, there is a
more visible upgrade in copper foil (vs glass cloth) taking place in Trainium 3 (2026),
while its OAM (open accelerator module) in Trainium 4 (2027) will likely be upgraded to
M8-grade CCL based on low Dk2 cloth. Simultaneously, we also expect a migration

toward HDI (high-density interconnect) from HLC (high-layer count), with regard to the
PCB production for the UBB (universal baseboard) in TPU v9 and Trainium 4.

Along with the data-rate migration toward 800G and beyond (from 100/200G) for optical
module, PCB structure evolves from conventional tenting design toward more
complicated mSAP/SAP (semi-additive process) with higher layer count, enabling finer
L/S (line/space) and smaller pitch (Exhibit 9). To elaborate, L/S of 3.2T optical module
should shrink materially to 15/15μm from 60/60μm at 800G, and it was 125/125um for

100G generation. Simultaneously, the layer count would also increase to up to layers for
3.2T products, vs 10-layer in 400G generation.
Amid the backdrop of rapid spec migration, major PCB suppliers are accelerating
capacity expansion to capture the burgeoning demand in high-end optical module
(Exhibit 10), and these include Avary under ZDT (Zhen Ding Technology), Founder, SCC
(Shennan Circuits), Unimicron and Compeq. While Unimicron remains the biggest

supplier of optical module PCB (printed circuit board) in 2H26, we flag Avary and SCC
could potentially become the other major suppliers in 2027, given their more aggressive
capacity increase.

Expect further ASP uplift driven by mix & cost inflation 
We expect CCL (for PCB) names’ revenue growth to be fueled by continuous capacity 
expansion and ASP uplifts, and these include EMC, TUC, Shengyi Tech and Kingboard 
Laminates among our coverage. The ASP increase, in our view, will be driven by both 
price hike on a like-for-like basis (given the cost push) and mix improvements to higher 
CCL grades, and we note the former/latter represent bigger boost to the near-
term/long-term ASP. To elaborate, the difference in near-term vs long-term boost can 
be explained by 1) a shorter production cycle time of less than 1-2 weeks by CCL makers 
which normally carry less than a quarter of inventory; and 2) a continuous spec 
advancement in CCL material grade given the requirement in data-transmission.  
In Exhibit 11-13, we decipher the revenue growth by different CCL names based on the 
planned capacity expansion and ASP uplift, as well as the assumption of 95%/90%/90% 
capacity utilization rate by EMC/TUC/Shengyi Tech in 2028. In our view, the implied ASP 
growth of 4%/11%/18% by respective names look conservative, and we see room for 
further upside in the case of continuous cost inflation from the raw materials (e.g. glass 
cloth, copper foil). Further, we believe a smaller magnitude of ASP increase by EMC (vs 
TUC/Shengyi Tech) in 2028 can be explained by its already significant exposure to high-
end CCL market in 2027. For reference, we present the CCL ASP by each grade with the 
figure for M4-grade indexed to 100 in Exhibit 20, and the gap of ASP by each grade 
remains quite meaningful, in our view.  
Regarding the CCL mix by grade, we break down the revenue contribution by different 
grades to each company throughout 2025-28, and we compare the numbers to their 
gross margins in Exhibit 14-19. When comparing the gross margin, we carve out the 
revenue mix from M7-grade and above CCL, given that M6-grade is already in supply-
demand balance (or slightly oversupply) situation from late-2025.

### Page 8

Among the three names, we expect EMC to remain the industry leader with 
73%/85%/92% revenue mix from M7-grade and above CCL in 2026/27/28, which helps 
strengthen its gross margin to 35.8%/36.7% in 2027/28 (vs 34% in 2026). TUC will 
follow suit with 43%/47%/53% revenue mix from M7-grade and above CCL in 
2026/27/28, with gross margin edging toward 32.6%/33.7% in 2027/28 (vs 30.4% in 
2026). As for Shengyi Tech, we expect 28%/35%/57% revenue mix from M7-grade and 
above CCL in 2026/27/28, and its gross margin (in CCL business) is expected to inch up 
to 31%/31.8 in 2027/28 (vs 30.7% in 2026).

Regarding the ASP uplifts by firm in 2027, we further decode into the factors of cost 
inflation and mix enhancement as shown in Exhibit 21. To simply put, we expect a 
smaller boost to EMC (vs TUC/Shengyi Tech) from cost inflation of raw materials and 
the corresponding pass-through, given its relatively lower revenue exposure to low-/mid-
end CCL market, where CCL makers pass through the cost more frequently upon 
squeezed margin profile. Yet, on the other hand, EMC (in terms of ASP) is expected to 
benefit more from the enhanced mix in 2027, thanks to the further ramp of M8/M8.5-
grade CCL as well as the penetration increase of M9 and PTFE-based solutions.  
Year-to-date, we believe there is already 40%+/20%+ price hike across low-/high-end 
CCL, and we do not rule out the likelihood of further price increase given the (see report) 
expectations of continuously rising copper price into late-2026 and 2027 (Exhibit 22). 
There was a price hike of VSP (very smooth profile) foil, effective from July 2026 by 
Mitsui Kinzoku given the demand strength and likely to be followed suit by a Taiwanese 
supplier (Co-Tech), based on our checks with supply chain. On the glass cloth side, we 
note the potential pricing upside from low Dk2 cloth given the supply shortage, while the 
price of low-end E-glass cloth (such as 7628 type) remains on the uptrend (Exhibit 23) 
and may reach CNY15-16 (per msq) by end-2026. In our view, all of these point to a 
benign pricing outlook for CCL makers.  
To pinpoint the benefits from price increase by CCL makers, we conduct a scenario 
analysis to each name’s 2027 bottom-line as shown in Exhibit 24. In short, we expect 
1.2-1.6% upside to the net income estimates coming from every 1ppt price hike, if such 
price increase purely reflects cost inflation. On the other hand, we expect 3.3-5.1% 
upside to the net income estimates, if the price hike is purely driven a supply shortage 
(which enables CCL makers to raise the ASP proactively). Further on Shengyi Tech, we 
assume that its PCB business (Shengyi Electronics) can also pass on the cost increase 
(from CCL side), with same magnitude to its ASP.  
For Kingboard Laminates, its vertical integration significantly enhances its earnings 
sensitivity to price hikes, as most of its key raw materials are produced in-house. 
Notably, price hikes in glass fiber cloth can translate almost entirely into profit, given in-
house production of yarn. Our analysis suggests that 100% increase in CCL prices could 
drive c.28ppt gross margin expansion for KBL, and more than 200% growth at the EPS 
level in 2026. This assumes that future price hikes are primarily driven by cloth 
tightness, while input costs for copper foil and resin remain relatively stable.

Sector view: stay positive on industry leaders  
We stay constructive on the CCL for PCB area, given the ongoing spec upgrades and 
robust demand growth into 2027-28, and we believe the worries from CPO (co-packaged 
optics) proliferation are either overstated or distant, as the mass adoption of CPO to 
replace NPO (near-packaged optics) or pluggable optical modules will not take place until 
2029/30, at earliest.  
Currently, we see extended lead-time (of production) toward 2-3 months by high-end 
CCL makers in 2H26 (vs 1-2 months seen in 1H26), while low/mid-end suppliers’ lead 
time is also extended to 1-2 months (7-10 days before). We also believe the current 
valuation by EMC (21-22x 2027E P/E) and TUC (17-18x 2027E P/E) looks compelling, 
given that the numbers do not seem lofty when compared with the historical trading 
range of 1) 15-20x before mid-2023; and 2) normally above 20x after mid-2023 when 
the strong demand from AI server starts to emerge. As for Kingboard Laminates, it is 
traded at 8x 2027E P/E, below historical average of 10x. 
Among our coverage, we highlight Buy-rated EMC, given its technology leadership in 
high-end CCL area along with diversified customer profile across GPU and ASIC 
accounts, as well as TUC, given its (see report) aggressive capacity expansion into 2027 
and beyond. We also highlight Buy rating on Doosan Corp and Kingboard Laminates, 
given the former’s solid position in NVIDIA GPU and Amazon switch, as well as the 
latter’s robust earnings expansion amid CCL price hike and rising high-end contribution 
starting 2027. As for Shengyi Tech, we remain relatively conservative, given its relatively 
slower expansion in CCL capacity along with a milder growth in the PCB business.

EMC (2383 TT; Buy; PO NT$8,200) 
We reckon EMC’s potential share loss in Rubin Ultra switch board (tray), given the 
competition in PTFE-based (hybrid) solutions (vs M9-Q), as well as the ongoing debate 
between PTFE and quartz-glass/hydrocarbon resin solutions into M10-grade CCL. We 
also note that M9-Q (where EMC has more notable technology leadership) is likely to be 
adopted in backplane by NVIDIA, no matter if it will initially be seen in Rubin Ultra or 
Feynman generation. Net-net, we believe the impact on bottom-line (if any) from 
aforementioned headwinds should be confined to LSD (low-single-digit)%, considering 
1) 80-90k (per month) of relevant demand by NVIDIA’s switch board vs EMC’s capacity 
of ~6/9mn in 2026/27; and 2) the demand backfill from other high-end projects across 
AMD and other ASIC customers.  
Looking ahead, we see further expansion plan beyond 1H28 as a catalyst, if it is 
announced. In addition to the CCL for PCB business, we note that substrate CCL 
business boasts at least 50-60% GM (gross margin), and EMC could expand relevant 
capacity to 1.2mn sheet /month which could help expedite its market-share gain toward 
~5% (vs 1-2% in 2025). We raise 2026/27/28E EPS by 5%/11%/14% after penciling in 
stronger revenue growth and gross margin profile, while our PO stays at NT$8,200 as 
we apply a lower P/E multiple of 30x (was 34x), with the same valuation period of 2H27-
1H28E, mainly reflecting the overhang from competition in PTFE-based solutions.

TUC (6274 TT; Buy; PO NT$2,600) 
Looking into 2027, we flag multiple positives from spec advancements for TUC, 
including 1) the potentially partial adoption of M9-grade CCL by 1.6T networking switch, 
where TUC’s solution is based on low Dk3 glass cloth; 2) the upgrade to M8-grade CCL 
for LEO (low earth orbit); and 3) the migration toward M8-grade material by Vera CPU 
(central processing unit). Meanwhile, we share the view that hybrid solution from PTFE-
based CCL may last toward M10-grade. Demand from high-end optical module (800G 
and above) could have remained underestimated at current stage, as we believe relevant 
TAM (total addressable market) for CCL could reach NT$30bn+, which represents close 
to 50% of TUC’s total revenue in 2026. In terms of Thailand operations, we believe it 
could turn breakeven or profitable from 3Q26, thanks to the smooth ramp of 
capacity/output to pair with the robust demand growth outlook from customers.  
We raise 2026/27/28E EPS by 7%/11%/7% after baking in the assumptions of stronger 
revenue growth and slightly enhanced GM profile as well as operating leverage. Our PO 
stays at NT$2,600, based on a lower P/E multiple of 27x (was 30.5x) and an unchanged 
valuation period of 2H27-1H28E. We attribute the cut in valuation multiple to the likely 
growing traction by PTFE-based solutions that serve as other alternatives in the high-
end CCL market.

Shengyi Technology (600183 CH; U/P; PO CNY99) 
We believe revenue contribution from PTFE-based CCL could reach ~10% based on the 
assumption of US$300-400 ASP per piece, and we expect the ramp of such business to 
bolster the overall margin profile thanks to ~50% GM by relevant solutions. Yet we also 
note that the firm’s capacity for M6-grade and above CCL remains relatively limited at 
~10% mix currently, and further capacity ramp will be dependent on the progress in 
Thailand and Songshan Lake (for M7-M9), after its Jiangxi phase 2 capacity became fully 
loaded after 1H26.  
We raise 2026/27/28E EPS by 29%/21%/18% after baking in 2Q26 results and the 
assumptions of stronger revenue growth and margin profiles, which also reflect its 
further share gain in NVIDIA’s AI server system/platforms. Our PO goes to CNY99 (from 
CNY83), same based on 24x 2027E P/E, and the volatility risk rating is changed to C 
from B.

Valuation discussion  
Both Buy-rated EMC and TUC are expected to post stronger EPS growth toward 2028 
along with a higher dividend yield. However, they trade at lower P/E multiples when 
compared with peers’ average in CCL industry. As for P/B multiples, the premium 
valuation by these two names, in our view, can be explained by much higher ROE 
expected in 2027-28. On the other hand, our more conservative view on Shengyi can be 
supported by its more stretched valuation (for both P/E and P/B) vs milder EPS growth 
and lower dividend yield, when compared with the peers’ average. Overall, we believe the 
(see report) overhang from CPO proliferation is already largely reflected in the valuation 
of CCL names, which we believe also renders an attractive opportunity for EMC/TUC, 
considering their solid earnings growth ahead fueled by continuous mix upgrades and 
more aggressive capacity expansion.

Price objective basis & risk 
Doosan Corp (DOSAF) 
Our PO of W1,800,000 is derived from SOTP valuation, which implies a fair market cap 
of W32tn = W20tn for core businesses (based on 20x 2027-28E P/E, which is in line with

### Page 22

the average of CCL peers) + W12tn in listed equity investments (Doosan Enerbility, 
Doosan Robotics, and Oricom) + net debt (breakeven) + other investments (W0.25tn, 
mostly non-listed affiliates). Our W1,800,000 PO implies 8x 2027-28E P/B, which 
appears much higher than the 2023-25 average of 4x, as well as a relatively high P/E 
(31x based on 2027-28E EPS) versus other Korea Holdco companies (5-10x). Key reason 
behind our relatively higher fair multiples compared to its historical average and its 
peers should be Doosan's rapidly growing CCL business thanks to its dominant market 
share position within NVIDIA.

Upside risks: (1) further market share gains in high-end CCL for major US hyperscalers, 
(2) a more favorable CCL pricing environment driven by sustained demand growth and 
tight industry supply, (3) incremental demand opportunities from emerging technologies 
such as co-packaged optics (CPO) and other networking equipment, and (4) faster-than-
expected capacity ramp-up.

Downside risks: (1) a slowdown in AI infrastructure spending or cuts to US hyperscaler 
capex, which could weaken demand for high-end CCL used in AI servers and networking 
equipment, (2) intensifying competition and pricing pressure in the advanced CCL 
market, and (3) potential delays in Doosan's CCL capacity expansion. 
Elite Material (ETMCF) 
Our PO of NT$8,200 is based on 30x 2H27-1H28E P/E, at the mid-to-high-end of its 
historical range of 6-34x since 2016. In our view, the multiple is appropriate, given the 
market-share expansion in server/networking, where we see structural growth in 
addressable market.

Upside risks are (1) share gain at substrate CCL, (2) stronger 5G smartphone shipment, 
(3) greater 5G smartphone spec upgrades, and (4) favorable FX and raw material cost 
change.

Downside risks are (1) share loss at iPhone, (2) keener competition in high-end CCL, and 
(3) unfavorable FX and raw material cost change. 
Kingboard Laminates (KGBLF) 
We set our PO at HK$65, on 13x 2H26-1H27E P/E. 13x is around +0.5SD of KBL's 
historical trading range, and matches with its last earnings upcycle in 2021 (led by e-
glass price hike) while also reflects rising mix from high-end AI-related deliveries.

Downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected CCL price hike, (2) better-than-expected 
industry supply of upstream materials esp. cloth, (3) slower-than-expected AI server 
demand, and (4) slower-than-expected external glass fiber cloth sales.

Upside risks: (1) stronger-than-expected CCL price hike, (2) slower-than-expected 
industry supply of upstream materials esp. cloth, (3) stronger-than-expected AI server 
demand, and (4) faster-than-expected external glass fiber cloth sales. 
Shengyi (DGUNF) 
Our PO of CNY99 is based on 24x 2027E P/E, close to the mid-to-high-end range of the 
historical average of 10-35x in 2017-2024. In our view, such valuation multiple is backed 
by operating margin and ROE of 23% and 37% in 2026-28E (vs 11% and 12% in 2023-
25).

Upside risks to our price objective are: (1) better macro conditions, (2) faster 5G 
deployment, (3) less-intensive competition, and (4) favorable FX and raw material cost 
changes.

### Page 23

Downside risks are: (1) worse macro conditions, (2) slower 5G deployment, (3) more 
severe competition, and (4) unfavorable FX and raw material cost changes. 
Taiwan Union Technology Corporation (TWUNF) 
Our PO NT$2,600 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E, which is at the mid-to-high-end of 
the historical trading range of 8-31x during 2016-2026. In our view, the valuation 
multiple is justified, given operating margin and ROE expansion toward 26% and 63% in 
2026-28E (vs 13% and 16% in 2023-25) along with robust free-cash flow generation in 
over the same period.

Downside risks are 
1) Fluctuation in raw material prices, which could lead to a more volatile gross margin 
for TUC in the near term 
2) More intensified competition in CCL, which could pressure TUC's overall margin 
profile 
3) Adoption of new type of upstream material (e.g., quartz), which could bring business 
uncertainty for the names in CCL industry, including TUC 
4) Worse-than-expected execution by the company, which could cause market-share loss 
and/or decreased business traction with customers

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 -  Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment rating 
of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, the 0-
12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a stock 
should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst ’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation). 
Investment rating Total return expectation (within 12-month period of date of initial 
rating) 
Ratings dispersion guidelines for coverage clusterR2 
Buy ≥ 10% ≤ 70% 
Neutral ≥ 0% ≤ 30% 
Underperform N/A ≥ 20% 
R2Ratings dispersions may vary from time to time where BofA Global Research believes it better reflects the investment prospects of stocks in a Coverage Cluster. 
INCOME RATINGS, indicators of potential cash dividends, are: 7 - same/higher (dividend considered to be secure), 8 - same/lower (dividend not considered to be secure) 
and 9 - pays no cash dividend.  Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two or more analysts sharing a common industry, sector, 
region or other classification(s). A stock ’s coverage cluster is included in the most recent BofA Global Research report referencing the stock.

Largan Precision  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 Results Miss on Lower
GPM, but FAU/CPO
Development Well on Track
Largan reported 3Q26 net income of NT$5.182bn, down 27% YoY.
The company reported a 11% YoY revenue decline, to NT$15.651bn; gross margin
declined YoY and QoQ to 42.2% in 3Q26, lower than expected due to lower new-
project yields and structural green electricity cost hikes.
Management expects shipments to be flat MoM in October and decline

sequentially in November as order momentum is weaker-than-expected.
FAU/CPO development well on track: First FA  project passed customer
verification; expanding from FA into FAU, including coupling and potential FAU-to-
carrier assembly, with a dedicated new facility expected to support mass production
from around mid-2027. We maintain our long-term positive view on Largan's FAU/
CPO opportunities.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Largan Precision (3008.TW)
Base case: Our price target is based on an SOTP valuation, comprising the traditional business
and potential FAU opportunity. We value the traditional business using a residual income
model, consistent with our Greater China Hardware Tech coverage, assuming an 8.5% cost of
equity, a 7% net profit CAGR over 2026-36E and a 3% terminal growth rate. We separately

value the FAU business, assuming a 40% market share in 2028.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected high-end smartphone demand
n Faster-than-expected penetration of periscopes
n Stronger-than-expected CPO volume growth
Risks to Downside
n Greater-than-expected competition, which would erode Largan's ASP and gross margin
n Weaker-than-expected smartphone demand, especially for high-end models
n Market share gains and more pricing pressure from Sunny

n Weaker-than-expected CPO volume growth

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

3Q26/4Q26 ABF、BT 稼動率持續向上，預期帶動營運成長 
南電 3Q26 主要成長動能來自於 ABF/BT，ABF 受惠 1.6T 高階交換器晶片及
AI ASIC 專案陸續放量，高階產品規格升級亦帶動平均銷售單價提升，其中
1.6T 交換器載板 ASP 相較前一代 800G 產品高出一倍以上。BT 載板則持續
受惠記憶體需求強勁。樹林廠及錦興廠的 ABF 與 BT 載板產能均已接近全面
滿載，昆山 ABF 稼動率 60%–70%以上，進入 4Q26 有望趨於滿載。本中心
預估 3Q/4Q26 營收 163.8/188.5 億元，季增 20.6%/15.1% ，毛利率
30%/32.3%，稅後獲利 35/46.3 億元，EPS 5.43/7.16 元。 
玻纖布、CCL 持續漲價，預期 4Q26–2027 年載板價格上漲 
經本中心供應鏈訪查了解，高階 BT 材料已呈明顯供不應求，使部分 BT 料
件 Lead time 甚至拉長至 70 週以上；ABF 隨新世代 AI 載板朝 20 層以上
Build-up 結構發展，單顆 ABF 膜使用量同步提高，Ajinomoto 供應亦趨緊；
玻纖布方面， Nittobo 新增玻纖布供給已大致完成客戶分配，短期缺口難以
明顯緩解。隨單顆載板面積放大，每張 Panel 可排入的產品數量將快速下降，
持續消耗載板廠商有效產能。綜合來看，本輪載板漲價由規格升級、單顆材
料用量增加與高階有效產能不足共同驅動，預期 ABF/BT 載板於 4Q26 至
2027 年仍具持續漲價空間。 
2027 年營運動能明顯增強，給予買進評等，目標價 1,700 元 
南電於 8M26 董事會通過 468 億元資本支出計畫，主要鎖定大尺寸、高層數、
高精密度 IC 載板。在高階產品比重提升、稼動率維持高檔及報價上調帶動
下，預期 ABF/BT 毛利率可望持續改善，整體營運動能於 2027 年明顯增強。
本中心估計 2026/2027 年南電營收 599.8/1,095.4 億元，年增 49.3%/82.6%，
毛利率 26.9%/43.4%，EPS 18.11/56.78 元。本中心給予南電買進評等，以
2027 年 EPS，30 倍本益比推得目標價 1,700 元。

元大觀點 
⧫  
台灣載板產能接近全面滿載，昆山 ABF 稼動率提升至 60%–70%且
4Q26 有望滿載，預估 3Q/4Q26 營收季增 20.6%/15.1%。 
⧫  
規格升級與高階 產品及上游原料 產能不足共同驅動載板漲價，預期
ABF/BT 載板於 4Q26 至 2027 年仍具持續漲價空間。 
⧫  
高階產品比重提升、稼 動率維持高檔及報價上調，給予 南電 買進評
等，目標價 1,700 元，以 2027 年 EPS 56.78 元及 30 倍本益比推估。

營運分析 
3Q26 ABF、BT 稼動率持續向上，預期帶動營運成長 
南電 3Q26 營收 163.8 億元，季增 20.6%，年增 49.3%，主要成長動能仍來自於 ABF/BT，ABF 載
板方面，受惠 1.6T 高階交換器晶片及 AI ASIC 專案陸續放量，除出貨量持續增加外，高階產品規
格升級亦帶動平均銷售單價提升。其中，1.6T 交換器載板 ASP 相較前一代 800G 產品高出一倍以
上，隨高階產品占比提升，為營收與獲利成長的推力。BT 載板則持續受惠 DRAM 與 NAND Flash
等記憶體需求強勁，加上上游原材料成本上漲逐步反映於產品報價，使 BT 載板呈現量價齊揚。毛
利率預估為 30%，營業利益 43.7 億元，季增 50.6%，年增 604.1%，稅後獲利 35 億元，季增 55%，
年增 383.6%，EPS 5.43 元。

台灣廠全面滿載，昆山 ABF 稼動率快速回升 
產能利用率方面，目前台灣主要載板廠區維持高檔運作，其中樹林廠及錦興廠的 ABF 與 BT 載板產
能均已接近全面滿載，顯示高階載板及記憶體相關需求仍相當強勁。同時，昆山 ABF 廠則為後續
營運改善的重要來源。隨美系網通大廠新專案自 7M26 正式進入量產，昆山 ABF 稼動率已由 2Q26
約 50%快速提升至 60%–70%，進入 4Q26 有望趨於滿載。伴隨稼動率提高及良率改善，昆山廠毛
利率亦顯著提升，預期後續貢獻幅度將提升。PCB 業務，稼動率正由 70%–75%向上，主因 AI 伺
服器板與記憶體模組板需求驅動。綜合上述，預期 4Q26 營收 188.5 億元，季增 15.1%，年增
68.8%，毛利率 32.3%，營業利益 55.1 億元，季增 26.1%，年增 472.1%，稅後獲利 46.3 億元，
季增 32%，EPS 7.16 元

玻纖布、 CCL 等價格仍持續漲價，預期 4Q26-2027 年載板價格上漲 
隨 AI GPU 、ASIC、高速交換器及 HBM 等應用快速成長，IC 載板規格持續朝大尺寸、高層數、細
線路、低翹曲及高耐熱發展，帶動材料由一般電子級產品轉向特種玻纖布、高階 BT 樹脂、ABF 增
層膜等高規材料。經本中心近期供應鏈訪查及日本材料展資訊整理，三菱瓦斯化學（ MGC）指出，
過去 BT 載板主要應用於 DRAM、NAND Flash 及消費性 IC，但隨 HBM、AI ASIC 、網通晶片與
CPO 導入，材料須進一步滿足低 CTE、高耐熱及高速電性需求，高階 BT 與傳統產品的規格差距
持續擴大，目前高階 BT 材料已呈明顯供不應求，部分料件 Lead time 甚至拉長至 70 週以上。玻纖
布方面，Nittobo 目前產能維持 100%滿載，雖規劃 2027–2028 年新增產能，但新增供給已大致完
成客戶分配，短期缺口難以明顯緩解。ABF 方面，隨新世代 AI 載板朝 20 層以上 Build-up 結構發
展，單顆 ABF 膜使用量同步提高，Ajinomoto 供應亦趨緊；同時 Resonac 相關載板材料報價已調
升約 20%–30%。綜合來看，本輪載板漲價並非單純景氣循環，而是規格升級、單顆材料用量增加
與高階有效產能不足共同驅動，預期 ABF／BT 載板於 4Q26 至 2027 年仍具持續漲價空間。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第4 頁，共 19頁  
圖 3：載板面積及層數增加亦在 Ajinomoto 未來載板發展藍圖

資料來源：Ajinomoto，元大投顧整理

南電此前已宣布 468 億元資本支出，為創立以來單筆最大投資 
南電於 8M26 董事會通過新一輪資本支出計畫，總投資金額達 468 億元，為公司成立以來最大單筆
投資案，規模亦超過 2018–2022 年上一輪擴產期間累計資本支出。其中約 110 億元投入廠房土建，
358 億元用於設備採購，新廠定位為具備 Class 100 無塵室等級的高階智慧工廠，主要鎖定大尺寸、
高層數、高精密度 IC 載板，以及 CoWoS、CoPoS 等先進封裝衍生需求。顯示此次投資並非既有
產線單純擴充，而是針對下一世代高階載板重新建置生產平台。整體而言 ，廠區調配方面，錦興廠
P2 升級已於 1H26 完成，主要提升自動化程度與無塵室規格，以因應大型、高階載板生產；樹林
廠則因應 2027 年美系客戶 Server CPU 需求成長，陸續將部分既有成熟產品轉往昆山生產，以釋
放台灣高階產能供新產品使用。

展望 2026-2027 年，ABF/BT 料源及載板的產能供應缺口將推升載板廠商營運 
AI 需求正在從「訓練」往「推論」延伸，這會讓晶片種類變多、數量變大，進而同時推升 IC 載板
的量與規格。此前從 Deep Learning 、Perceptual AI 一路到 Generative AI、Agentic AI，未來再往
Physical AI 發展。訓練（Learning）主要依賴大型模型運算，因此核心晶片以高效能 GPU 為主；
但到了推論（Inference）階段，更重視即時反應速度、功耗與成本，因此除了 GPU 之外，CPU 與
ASIC 的使用比例會明顯提高。展望 2026/2027 年，預計南電載板稼動率持續改善，其中 ABF 與
BT 均已進入高檔運作。ABF 方面，台灣樹林及錦興廠稼動率已接近滿載；昆山廠則為 2H26 稼動
率回升的主要來源，受惠美系網通客戶及中國客戶量產，ABF 稼動率由 2Q26 約 50%快速提升至
3Q26 的 60%–70%，預期 4Q26 在供給持續吃緊下，可進一步提升，2027 年初亦將因應美系客戶
Server CPU 需求快速成長。BT 載板供需則更加緊張，除現有 DRAM、SSD 相關需求持續強勁，
另一方面，部分同業逐步將 BT 產能轉向 ABF，韓系供應商亦優先支應集團內需求，市場有效供給
持續收縮，景碩亦表示稼動率也將逐步向上，預期 2027 年 BT 仍將維持高稼動率。 
綜合上述，本中心估計 2026/2027 年南電營收 599.8/1095.4 億元，年增 49.3%/82.6%，毛利率
26.9%/43.4%，營業利益 141.3/442.3 億元，年增 612.7%/213.1%，稅後獲利 117/366.9 億元，年
增 501.2%/213.5%，EPS 18.11/56.78 元。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第5 頁，共 19頁  
產業概況  
AI 需求增溫造成 T-glass 玻纖布供應吃緊，助益近期 BT 載板報價調整 
低熱膨脹係數（Low CTE ）玻璃纖維布，簡稱 T-glass，為高階 AI ABF 載板及高階 BT 載板重要原
料，由 Nittobo(日東紡)獨家供應，並由日系廠商 Resonac(ABF 載板 CCL 主要供應商)、三菱瓦斯
(BT 載板 CCL 主要供應商)採用。由於高階 AI 晶片需求增溫帶動高階 AI ABF 載板出貨成長，造成
T-glass 供應吃緊並連帶使高階 BT 載板 CCL(終端用途包含記憶體控制器、手機 Ap、Sip 等)交貨
期拉長，T-glass 玻纖布供應正常時高階 BT 載板 CCL 交貨期約 8-10 週，後因供應吃緊造成交貨期
拉長至 16-20 週，目前因玻纖布供應更為緊張已無告知交貨期。 
經供應鏈調查，台系載板廠已於 2025 年 7 月開始針對部分採用 T-glass 之 BT 載板產品調漲價格，
此外 BT 載板亦有載板廠進行價格調漲，以反應高階玻纖布供應日漸緊缺，以及年初以來銅價、金
價等原物料成本上漲壓力。由於 BT 載板產業長期以來供過於求，且當前高階 AI 晶片普遍採用
ABF 載板，多數載板廠此前 BT 業務毛利率較為偏低，淡季時季毛利率多有承壓。本中心認為漲價
趨勢可延續，將有助載板廠獲利改善。

圖 6：金價近一年走勢

資料來源：Cmoney、元大投顧整理

中長期高階 ABF 載板價格或能進一步調升 
受惠 AI 發展浪潮帶動 AI GPU 、AI ASIC 等 AI 晶片需求成長，AI ABF 載板需求正向。本中心推測
短期高階 AI ABF 載板因獲利水準足夠暫無調漲壓力，但中長期隨需求持續上升、載板面積增大、
T-glass 供應持續吃緊，AI ABF 載板產品台系廠商後續仍有進一步議價空間。目前欣興、南電、景
碩皆有耕耘高階 ABF 載板，例如欣興在美系 GPU Blackwell 市佔率預估為 30-40%，且為全球主要
AI ASIC 載板供應商；南電今年 800G 載板比重預期約 7%，包含美系、非美系等終端需求；景碩
則有望成為美系 AI GPU 載板第三供。此外部分載板廠因原物料上漲等成本壓力在 2Q25、3Q25 小
幅調整中低階 ABF 載板報價，本中心判斷若 T-glass 供應持續吃緊並排擠 E-glass 產能，則後續中
低階 ABF 載板價格有望進一步提升。

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第7 頁，共 19頁  
AI、ASIC、通用型強勢出擊，2026 年美系 CSP 資本支出估年增 91%  
在 ChatGPT 等 AI 應用陸續推向市場，各大業者紛紛開始研發自有超級大模型，包括 Gemini 
(Google)、Claude (Anthropic) 、Llama (Meta) 等，各大品牌開始大舉投入大型語言模型(LLM)市場，
且每一個產品的智慧皆持續提高，部分消費者也陸續從 ChatGPT 陣營轉移至 Gemini 陣營，反映
出消費者開始逐漸提升對 AI 的黏著度。根據 Grand View Research 調查，2024 年全球 AI 市場營
收約 2,792 億美元，預估 2030 年市場規模超過 3 兆美元，擁有超過 30% 的年複合增長率。 
面對這爆發性的長期成長趨勢，CSP 業者(Amazon、Microsoft、Google、Meta)在法說會上一致強
調，AI 投資已轉變為一項橫跨 2026 年的結構性戰略，CSP 大廠不僅持續擴大對 GB300 等次世代
AI 伺服器的採購，更將重點放在資料中心基礎設施的長期戰略規劃，以應對 AI 運算對電力及散熱
系統強勁成長的需求，確保未來數年內擁有足夠的能源與容量來支持持續迭代的大模型部署。本中
心認為，2026 年美國四大 CSP 資本支出將年增 91%，且將有一半的成長來自於資料中心建置，代
表了資料中心的不足及未來伺服器需求。明年不僅僅是 NVIDIA GB300 的天下，AWS、Google、
Meta ASIC 的崛起也將是一大關鍵，其中又以 Google TPU 需求最為強勁，已經有兩個客戶預計將
採購 TPU。 
另外，受惠於 AI 訓練與推論需求爆發的連帶效應，本中心預估 2026 年通用型伺服器出貨量將年增
23%，其中以存儲型伺服器需求最為強勁。隨著生成式 AI 應用普及和使用者數量暴增，CSP 等業
者必須不斷擴充容量，且不僅限於高階 GPU 伺服器，龐大的 AI 模型訓練和推論在使用狀態中將消
耗大量的數據(DATA)，使得存儲型伺服器的需求大增。

ASIC 進入高速成長期，GPU 需求仍維持擴張 
從 AI 晶片預期出貨來看，GPU 與 ASIC 需求均持續成長，但 2026 年後 ASIC 成長動能明顯加速。
Nvidia GPU 出貨量預估由 2025 年 565.7 萬顆增至 2026 年 650 萬顆、2027 年 850 萬顆，NVIDIA
加 AMD GPU 預估年增 17%及 34%；AMD 亦由 2025 年 90 萬顆增至 2027 年 180 萬顆，顯示通用
GPU 算力需求仍維持成長。 
相較之下，ASIC 受惠 CSP 加速自研晶片及導入規模擴大，總出貨量預估由 2025 年 380 萬顆大幅
增至 2026 年 725 萬顆、2027 年 1,360 萬顆，年增率達 91%及 88%，成長速度明顯高於 GPU。其
中 Google 仍為最大 ASIC 使用者， 2027 年用量預估達 800 萬顆，AWS 亦增至 320 萬顆，
Microsoft 及 Meta 同步擴大部署。我們認為，在資料中心電力逐漸成為算力擴張瓶頸下， CSP 將
更重視每瓦運算效能及整體運算成本， ASIC 針對特定運算工作的效率優勢有望推升滲透率，惟
GPU 憑藉通用性及軟體生態系優勢，仍將維持重要地位。

圖 9：GPU/ASIC 出貨量預估

資料來源：元大投顧

AI 晶片面積持續擴大，將使 ABF 載板用量大增 
隨著大型語言模型與生成式 AI 的算力需求快速攀升，AI 晶片的設計持續朝向更高運算密度與更大
面積發展。無論是 NVIDIA、AMD 或各家大廠的新世代加速器，都呈現晶粒尺寸擴大、功耗上升、
I/O 腳位數倍增的趨勢。在此背景下，ABF 載板的需求不僅以「量」擴張，更以「規格」升級為主
軸。為支撐大型 GPU/AI ASIC 的高頻寬連接與多晶片架構，ABF 層數、線寬線距、孔徑控制及翹
曲管理均需同步提升，帶動高端載板技術門檻不斷拉高。由於先進 AI 晶片的封裝面積更大、電源
與訊號整合更複雜，單顆晶片所需 ABF 載板面積與材料用量明顯增加。整體而言，AI 晶片規模擴
大正驅動 ABF 產業進入新一輪升級周期，高階需求將成為未來成長的主要動能。

### Page 10

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第10 頁，共 19頁  
獲利調整與股票評價  
給予南電買進評等，目標價 1700 元 
本中心維持 2026–2027 年載板廠商將持續受惠於高稼動率與漲價趨勢的看法不變。主因新世代高
階載板規格持續大型化，傳統 PC／NB 載板尺寸多約 27×27mm，而 Server CPU 、AI ASIC 及高
速交 換器 載板 已逐 步 放大 至 70–80mm 、100mm 以 上， 部分 大型 產品 甚 至 朝 140×140mm 、
150×150mm 發展，未來更大型先進封裝架構更可能逼近 200mm 等級。由於載板生產係以固定尺
寸 Panel 進行製造，隨單顆載板面積放大，每張 Panel 可排入的產品數量將快速下降，同時邊角無
法有效利用所造成的 Area Loss 亦明顯增加，此將持續消耗載板廠商有效產能，有助產業維持高稼
動率，並支撐 2026–2027 年載板報價向上。本中心估計 2026/2027 年南電營收 599.8/1095.4 億元，
年增 49.3%/82.6%，毛利率 26.9%/43.4%，營業利益 141.3/442.3 億元，年增 612.7%/213.1%，
稅後獲利 117/366.9 億元，年增 501.2%/213.5%，EPS 18.11/56.78 元。 
綜合上述，南電高階產品比重提升、稼動率維持高檔及報價上調帶動下，預期 ABF／BT 毛利率可
望持續改善，整體營運動能於 2027 年明顯增強。評價方面，南電歷史本益比交易區間約介於 6–36
倍；考量本輪 ABF／BT 供需結構改善、高階產品組合升級，以及稼動率快速提升所帶來的毛利率
擴張，本中心給予 2027 年 30 倍本益比評價，以 2027 年預估 EPS 推算目標價 1,700 元，並給予
買進投資建議。

公司簡介  
南亞電路板成立於 1997 年，前身為南亞塑膠自 1985 年開始營運的電路板事業部，1998 年正式獨立營運，並於 2006 年在
台灣證券交易所掛牌上市，為台塑集團旗下主要 PCB 與 IC 載板廠商，目前南亞塑膠持股約 67%。南亞電路板     年報
_8046 南電_2025 年報(1) 公司產品涵蓋傳統多層印刷電路板、HDI、軟硬複合板（Rigid-Flex），以及 ABF 與 PP／BT IC
載板，生產據點主要位於台灣錦興、樹林及中國昆山。南亞電路板 其中 IC 載板為公司主要高階產品布局，應用涵蓋伺服器
CPU、AI ASIC、高效能運算（HPC）、800G／1.6T 高速交換器、記憶體、SSD 控制晶片、SiP、行動裝置及車用電子等
領域；一般 PCB 則持續往 AI 伺服器、GPU 加速卡、伺服器與高階網通等高附加價值產品升級。    年報_8046 南電_2025
年報(1) 公司近年營運重心進一步向 AI 與 HPC 相關高階載板移動，受惠 AI 晶片朝大尺寸、高層數及高 I/O 規格發展，以及
CSP 持續擴大自研 ASIC 布局，南電持續強化高階 ABF 載板、1.6T 交換器及 AI 伺服器相關產品布局，並規劃新增高階載
板產能，以支應下一階段 AI 基礎建設需求。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第16 頁，共 19頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 南電整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低等風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於略為領先
的位置，在電子零組件行業的公司中排名略微領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 南電的整體曝險屬於低等水準，略為領先電子零組件行業的平均水準。  
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：南電在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等。公司訊息揭露能力非常優良，
提供投資人及市場充足的資訊。

註：A = 未調整之收盤價；B = 未調整之目標價； C = 依據股票股利與現金股利調整後之目標價。員工分紅稀釋影響未反映於 A、B 或 C。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

註：元大給予個股之目標價係依 12 個月投資期間計算。大中華探索系列報告並無正式之 12 個月目標價，其投資建議乃根據分析師報告中之指定期間分析
而得。

Doanh thu 2Q2026 tăng trưởng chậm 
nhưng biên lợi nhuận gộp vẫn được 
duy trì do tăng giá bán

Sản lượng tiêu thụ 6M2026 chững lại 
do tâm lý nhập hàng thận trọng và 
hoạt động xây dựng phục hồi chậm

Biên lợi nhuận gộp được dự báo sẽ 
thu hẹp cuối năm, phản ánh chi phí 
nguyên liệu cao hơn trong 1Q2026

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 166,100 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Doanh thu/LNST 2Q2026 đạt lần lượt 1,320/367 tỷ VND (+1%/+11% YoY). Lũy kế 
6M2026, doanh thu đạt 2,777 tỷ VND (+3% YoY) trong khi LNST ước đạt 671 tỷ VND 
(+9% YoY). Biên lãi gộp 6M2026 cải thiện đạt 47.0% (+240 bps YoY), chủ yếu do việc 
tăng giá bán giúp doanh nghiệp bù đắp được mức tăng của chi phí đầu vào.

Lũy kế 6M2026, sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP ước đạt 43,920 tấn, giảm 5% svck. Việc 
BMP thu hẹp các chương trình chiết khấu, với mức chiết khấu bình quân khoảng 
5.2% trong 2Q2026, làm giảm động lực nhập hàng của các đại lý và khiến sản lượng 
tiêu thụ chậm lại. Dù vậy, thị phần toàn quốc của BMP ước đạt 28% (+1 đpt svck) 
nhờ các doanh nghiệp khác trong ngành gặp khó khăn khi giá hạt nhựa tăng mạnh.

Biên lợi nhuận gộp của BMP dự kiến thu hẹp trong 2H2026 trước áp lực chi phí 
nguyên liệu đầu vào tăng. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi kỳ vọng mức độ ảnh hưởng sẽ thấp 
hơn so với các doanh nghiệp trong ngành do (1) các lô nguyên liệu giá cao từ 
1Q2026 đã được đưa vào sản xuất và tiêu thụ một phần trong 1H2026; (2) BMP chủ 
động nâng gấp đôi hàng tồn kho để ứng phó với biến động bất lợi của giá nguyên 
liệu; (3) hơn 90% nhu cầu nguyên liệu uPVC được cung ứng từ các đối tác trong 
nước như hệ sinh thái SCG tại Việt Nam, AGC Chemicals và LSP; và (4) công ty duy 
trì giá bán bình quân ở mức cao.

Dựa trên kết quả kinh doanh và định giá, chúng tôi khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP với cổ 
phiếu BMP, giá mục tiêu là 166,100 VND/cổ phiếu (tương ứng với mức tăng 14.3% 
so với giá đóng cửa ngày 23/09/2026).

Nhựa Bình Minh (BMP) là doanh nghiệp hàng đầu 
trong lĩnh vực sản xuất ống nhựa với thị phần 
dẫn đầu tại thị trường nội địa (27% vào cuối 
2023), thị trường chính tập trung tại khu vực 
miền Nam. BMP có 4 nhà máy với tổng công suất 
đạt 150,000 tấn/năm. 
Nguồn: BMP, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Kỳ vọng đà phục hồi của xây dựng dân dụng và bất động sản miền Nam sẽ thúc đẩy sản lượng tiêu 
thụ tăng trưởng trong 2026–2027. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Giá PVC được kỳ vọng sẽ hạ nhiệt và khả năng giữ giá bán đầu ra ở mức cao giúp BMP giữ biên lợi 
nhuận gộp ở nền cao.

### Page 4

4

Nhu cầu nhập hàng thận trọng và 
cắt giảm các đợt chiết khấu trong 
kỳ của BMP làm giảm sản lượng tiêu 
thụ trong 2Q2026

Sự hồi phục của nhu cầu xây dựng 
dân dụng và nguồn cung các dự án 
bất động sản tại miền Nam sẽ thúc 
đẩy tăng trưởng sản lượng 2H2026 
 Sản lượng tiêu thụ trong 2Q2026 ước đạt 20,100 tấn, giảm 11% svck, nâng tổng 
sản lượng tiêu thụ 6M2026 ước đạt 43,920 tấn, giảm 5% svck do các yếu tố bất 
lợi từ bên ngoài như (1) giá hạt nhựa tăng cao khiến thị trường dè chừng trong 
việc nhập hàng vì lo ngại nguồn cung bất ổn (2) BMP đã thu hẹp các chương 
trình chiết khấu so với 2025 ngay trước thời điểm xung đột bùng nổ, giúp tỷ lệ 
chi phí bán hàng/doanh thu về mức 11.3%, giảm 800 bps so với 1Q2026 ở mức 
19.3%, nhưng đồng thời cũng khiến sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP suy giảm do 
chính sách bán hàng thắt chặt hơn với các đối thủ có thị phần thấp hơn.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP dần hồi phục từ 3Q2026 khi nhu 
cầu nhập hàng của các đại lý cải thiện sau giai đoạn thận trọng trong nửa đầu 
năm. Về cuối năm, hoạt động xây dựng dự kiến tăng tốc khi điều kiện thi công 
thuận lợi hơn sau mùa mưa. Bên cạnh đó, việc triển khai các dự án nhà ở tại 
TP.HCM và các tỉnh lân cận được kỳ vọng hỗ trợ nhu cầu ống nhựa trong giai 
đoạn tới. Theo đó, chúng tôi dự phóng sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP lần lượt đạt 
96,695/100,125/96,750 tấn trong 2026/2027/2028.

### Page 5

5

Lợi thế từ hệ sinh thái công ty mẹ 
SCG và đối tác Nhật Bản giúp BMP 
có nhiều dư địa để nâng cao hiệu 
quả hoạt động

Theo chia sẻ từ ban lãnh đạo, BMP có lợi thế vì nhập nguyên liệu từ công ty mẹ 
là hệ sinh thái SCG tại Việt Nam và đối tác Nhật Bản là AGC Chemicals Vietnam, 
giúp giảm được 5% thuế nhập khẩu và chi phí vận chuyển. Điều này giúp BMP 
hưởng lợi kép khi có thể tăng được giá bán theo thị trường nhưng nguyên vật 
liệu rẻ hơn so với các doanh nghiệp trong ngành. KBSV nhận thấy định hướng 
dài hạn của BMP là cân bằng lợi ích cổ đông và khách hàng, vì vậy việc giảm giá 
bán để giành thị phần không phải lựa chọn ưu tiên của doanh nghiệp mà là 
nâng cao tỷ suất lợi nhuận/sản phẩm thành phẩm.

KBSV dự báo biên lợi nhuận gộp thu hẹp trong 2H2026 khi chi phí nguyên liệu 
giá cao từ 1H2026 được phản ánh vào giá vốn, và hồi phục trong 2027–2028 
nhờ đặc tính giữ giá bán ít thay đổi của nhóm nhựa. Chúng tôi kỳ vọng giá PVC 
resin chịu áp lực giảm từ (1) nhu cầu xây dựng tại Trung Quốc còn yếu khi thị 
trường bất động sản chưa hồi phục rõ ràng; và (2) các nhà sản xuất giải phóng 
hàng tồn kho sau mùa mưa, làm tăng sản lượng hạt nhựa PVC resin. Theo đó, 
KBSV dự phóng biên lợi nhuận gộp của BMP lên 46.4%/46.7%/46.5% trong 
2026/2027/2028.

gộp của BMP

### Page 1

MACROMICRO RESEARCH | FED WATCH
聯準會官員發⾔整理｜ 2026/10/12 （台灣時間）
涵蓋期間：近  14 天，美東時間  9/28 ⾄  10/11 。共整理  14 位官員，其中  9 位為  2026 年票委、 5 位為⾮
票委，並標注發⾔⽇期與鷹鴿⽴場。最後提出對政策路徑與資產配置的結論。
鷹鴿光譜⼀覽
粗體為  2026 年票委。
最鷹 Lorie Logan
鷹派 Michael Barr、Christopher Waller、 Alberto Musalem 、 Jeff Schmid
偏鷹 Neel Kashkari、Beth Hammack、Lisa Cook、 Austan Goolsbee
中性 John Williams、Philip Jefferson、 Mary Daly
偏鴿 （本期無）
未表態 Michelle Bowman、 Susan Collins
• 票委陣營：主張續升的票委有  3 位： Lorie Logan 估計⾄少再升  50 個基點， Michael Barr 認為需要「重新校

準」政策， Christopher Waller 預期再升息但不必連續會議動作。 Neel Kashkari 、 Beth Hammack 、 Lisa Cook
⽅向偏鷹，但對  10 ⽉時點保持開放； John Williams 與  Philip Jefferson 則明⾔「沒有急迫性」、需要更多時
間。 9 位發⾔票委沒有⼈主張降息，多數落在「⽅向向上、時點不急」， Kevin Warsh 近兩週未公開談政
策。
• ⾮票委鷹派：Alberto Musalem 主張  6–9 個⽉內再升、 Jeff Schmid 直⾔  AI 已是主要通膨推⼿、 Austan
Goolsbee 形容通膨久⾼「是在玩⽕」，⾮票委整體⽐票委更鷹；但他們今年沒有投票權，影響在於⽀撐  12
⽉與  2027 年初的升息定價，⽽⾮  10 ⽉的決策。
• 結論：市場已跟著票委的耐⼼訊號轉向。 CME 的  10 ⽉升息機率從  9/28 的  70.9% 降⾄  10/9 的  17.7% ，按兵
不動升⾄  82.3% ；但  12 ⽉累計升息機率仍有  82.7% ， 9 ⽉會議紀要也顯⽰多數官員認為年底前再升⼀次合

適。 10 ⽉暫停是延後，不是升息循環結束。
⼀、 2026 年票委（ 9 位，依鷹⾄鴿排列）

### Page 2

票委 最鷹
Lorie Logan   達拉斯  Fed 總裁
發⾔重點
10/1   於達拉斯  Fed 「 Voices of the Eleventh District 」活動開場致詞：估計利率⽬標區間需「再上調
50 個基點或更多」，⽬標是讓政策達到「溫和緊縮」。
10/1  9 ⽉升息是「重要的第⼀步」；⾄少再升幾碼，才能抵銷去年秋天共  75 個基點的「⾵險管理」
降息，「我們必須恢復物價穩定」。
10/1   通膨正朝  2.5% 左右前進，⽽⾮  2% ⽬標；失業率  4.1% 已接近多數估計的最低可持續⽔準，
勞動市場⼤致平衡。
10/1   ⻑天期殖利率近幾週明顯上升，較⾼的期限溢價可能拖慢經濟、減少所需的緊縮幅度；市場
本⾝也預期利率將⾛⾼。
10/2 、 10/6   先後在達拉斯  Fed 移⺠研討會致詞、於聖安東尼奧主持與前  BIS 總經理  Agustín
Carstens 的對談，均未⾒新的政策評論。
市場  10 年期美債殖利率當天盤中創  24 年新⾼後回落，收在  5.24% ； Bloomberg 以「需要更多升

息，但債市⾛勢可能幫上忙」解讀其發⾔。
票委 鷹派
Michael Barr  Fed 理事
發⾔重點
9/29   於底特律經濟俱樂部演講「經濟情勢與貨幣政策」：通膨達標的⾵險上升、勞動市場⾵險下
降，「需要重新校準政策」；基準情境下，可能還需要進⼀步調整政策，才能讓通膨及時回到⽬
標。
9/29   通膨已⾼於⽬標五年半；過去  20 個⽉只有  2 個⽉的核⼼  PCE 符合  2% ，「尚未看到及時回到
2% 的明確趨勢」，降溫進程被中東衝突與  AI 需求打亂。
9/29   勞動市場供需⼤致平衡，每⽉新增約  8 萬個職位、接近損益兩平，失業率  4.1% ；上半年
GDP 成⻑約  2% ，預期將略為加快。
9/29  AI 帶動晶⽚與相關設備價格飆升；未來  2–5 年最⼤的不確定性在⽣產⼒何時顯現、 AI 投資
重新定價與勞動替代，年輕勞⼯已出現早期跡象；持久的⽣產⼒提升可能推⾼中性利率，但「現
在判斷還太早」。
9/29   ⾼能源價格「仍在」，關稅的影響則可能已經減弱。

市場   同⽇  John Williams 表⽰不急， CME 的  10 ⽉升息機率由  9/28 的  70.9% 降⾄約  50% 。

發⾔重點
10/9   於密⻄根⼤學  John Leahy 紀念研討會致詞，追思其研究貢獻。
10/9   未對貨幣政策或經濟前景發表評論。
結論
數據更新⾄  2026-10-09 。
四⼤結論
1  10 ⽉按兵不動是基準情境， 12 ⽉再升⼀碼才是主路徑。 9 ⽉⾮農僅增  2.9 萬、失業率升⾄
4.2% 、時薪年增放緩⾄  3.0% ，給了  John Williams 、 Philip Jefferson 等票委等待的理由；但  9 ⽉會
議紀要顯⽰多數與會者認為年底前再升⼀次合適， Christopher Waller 也只是把升息的時點交給數
據決定。截⾄  10/9 ， CME 顯⽰  10 ⽉不動機率  82.3% 、 12 ⽉累計升息機率  82.7% 。投資上不宜為
降息預期定價， 3 個⽉期國庫券殖利率  4.25% 仍是等待資⾦的最佳停泊處，成⻑股的評價要靠盈
餘與現⾦流⽀撐，⽽⾮寬鬆預期。
2   暫停預期隨時可能逆轉，關鍵在油價與核⼼服務通膨。 10/9 布蘭特油價收在每桶  104.72 美元，

9 ⽉  ISM 服務業物價指數升⾄  74.0 、為  2022 年  7 ⽉以來新⾼，製造業物價指數  77.9 ，紐約  Fed
調查的⼀年期通膨預期升⾄  3.9% ，四項都指向通膨的第⼆輪效應。 10/14 公布的  9 ⽉  CPI 若核⼼
服務⾛強， 12 ⽉升息將被坐實、 10 ⽉升息機率也可能回升，這呼應  Lisa Cook 、 Philip Jefferson
對通膨擴散的警告。反向的條件是⾮農、零售與初領失業⾦同步轉弱，屆時衰退⾵險會取代通膨
⾵險，政策路徑才會轉向。
3   ⻑天期美債仍不宜承接，短天期國庫券是較佳的等待資⾦。 10 年期美債殖利率  10/5 收在
5.31% 、 10/7 盤中觸及約  5.36% 創  2002 年以來新⾼， 10/9 收在  5.24% ；同期  2 年期由  9/28 的
4.92% 回落⾄  4.80% ，顯⽰⻑端壓⼒主要來⾃期限溢價、財政與  AI 融資供給，⽽⾮政策路徑。
Alberto Musalem 估計  AI 帶動的資本需求約占  GDP 3%–4% 且將持續  5–10 年， Beth Hammack 更

直⾔殖利率上升無法取代升息。要等核⼼  PCE 連續降溫、⻑端殖利率不再創⾼、標售需求改善三
者同時出現，才是分批拉⻑存續期間的時機。
4   股票維持核⼼部位，但加碼⾨檻要提⾼；⿈⾦與美元各有分⼯。經濟仍在擴張、官員多認為勞
動市場撐得住升息，股票仍優於⻑債，但美股與  AI 供應鏈的加碼應以⾃由現⾦流、未履約訂單
（ RPO ）與資本報酬率驗證，避開依賴遠期現⾦流的⾼估值部位。⿈⾦  10/9 收在每盎司  4,216 美
元，適合維持  10%–15% 的分批避險部位，承擔財政與地緣⾵險，實質利率急升時暫緩加碼。美
元指數  10/9 為  102.21 、接近  10/5 創下的  52 週⾼點  102.54 ，保留美元短券作為流動性即可，不宜
因官員偏鷹就單邊押注美元⾛強。
近期觀察⽇曆
10/12 Beth Hammack 於  NABE 年會談經濟展望與貨幣政策

Document 264: Insurance costs hit the brakes Insurance payment growth is cooling, but the relief consumers are seeing depends heavily on who and where they are. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Insurance costs hit the brakes Insurance payment growth is cooling, but the relief consumers are seeing depends heavily on who and where they are. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

13028742     1

Economy 
Insurance costs hit the brakes 
16 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Property insurance (e.g., auto, homeowners, renters) payment growth has slowed considerably, according to Bank of America 
payments data. Median payments rose just 1% year-over-year (YoY) in 2026, easing further from the 6% growth seen in 2025, 
after a roughly 12% increase in 2024, suggesting that many consumers are getting some relief from rising insurance payments. 
• However, the moderation is uneven across consumers. Bank of America payments data suggests that Gen Z continues to 
experience insurance payment growth of nearly 6%, while increases for other generations are closer to 1% to 2%. Payments 
also rose nearly 1.5% for higher-income households but edged lower for those with lower incomes. 
• Lower accident rates, easing vehicle prices, and softer major-purchase growth may be reducing cost pressures, but geography 
remains a major dividing line. Florida metros recorded declines of roughly 7% to 8%, while California and several Northeastern 
metros continued to see relatively strong increases.

Insurance payments stabilize after years of painful growth 
The burden of higher property (e.g., auto, renters, homeowners) insurance payments may be easing for some. Notably, while the 
average payment increased 3% year-over-year (YoY), the median payment was only up 1%, according to Bank of America 
payments data. This suggests that most consumers have only seen a small increase in their premiums, though there may be a 
few outliers (Exhibit 1).  
The latest data stands in contrast to just two years ago in 2024, when most consumers saw their insurance payments surge 
about 12% YoY, after a nearly 8% YoY increase in 2023.   
Exhibit 1: Insurance payments have increased only modestly through August of this year, with growth peaking in 2024 
Median and average insurance payments (September to August yearly, YoY%)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Insurance payments by age show a similar pattern, with much stronger growth in 2024 across most generations followed by a 
cooling since then (Exhibit 2). Gen Z is the outlier here, with their payments still growing at a healthy rate, increasing almost 6% 
YoY for both 2026 and 2025, after a 7% increase in 2024. Meanwhile, Millennials and Baby Boomers experienced a 2% YoY 
increase so far this year, with Gen X only seeing around a 1% rise.

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Bank of America payments data by income provides even more insight. Higher-income households paid nearly 1.5% more in 
2026 than they did a year ago, while middle-income households saw only modest increases or none at all (Exhibit 3). Conversely, 
lower-income households actually paid slightly less so far this year than they did last year.  
Exhibit 3: Higher-income households are still paying more for insurance compared to last year, while lower earners are paying less 
Median insurance payments by income cuts (September to August yearly, YoY%)

Source: Bank of America internal data 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Fewer accidents and lower vehicles prices may be driving insurance payments down 
But why might insurance payments be declining at all? One thing to keep in mind is that insurance companies often raise rates 
to cover events that occurred in previous years to recover costs, while adjusting for changing risks.  
For example, the crash rate – the number of vehicle crashes per one million miles driven – suggests that there was a sharp rise 
in accidents in 2021, according to the Department of Transportation (Exhibit 4). Additionally, new and used vehicle prices 
increased significantly after the pandemic, according to Consumer Price Index (CPI) data from the Bureau of Labor Statistics 
(BLS) (Exhibit 5). So, insurers were not only facing more vehicle replacements due to a higher crash rate, but a higher cost to 
replace vehicles as well.

However, the crash rate mostly declined from 2021 to 2024, and the Department of Transportation reported that the number of 
accidents likely decreased again in 2025. Also, while the cost to maintain and repair vehicles has consistently grown since 2021, 
the price to replace vehicles peaked in 2022 and has eased since then – the latter likely a more important factor in insurance 
costs than the former. Additionally, while the CPI index for motor vehicle insurance has fallen 5% YoY, it’s possible that this may 
partially be due to rebates, according to BofA Global Research. So, the price figured into CPI may not be fully realized by the 
consumer. 
Exhibit 4: The rate of crashes is below pre- and post-pandemic

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Another factor in the divergence between a declining CPI and rising insurance payments in our data may be the growth in the 
amount of property people are insuring now. Notably though, this growth has eased in recent years. YoY growth in both new 
home and total vehicle sales peaked in mid-2023 and has grown modestly, or even declined, since (Exhibit 6). And given that 
these major purchases are still K-shaped (read more in Autos: In gear, but the “K” is still in the headlights and On the move: 
Renovation over relocation?), it could help to explain the divergence in insurance payment growth across income cohorts.  
Exhibit 6: Growth in new home and total vehicle sales peaked in mid-2023 due to a post-pandemic boom but has eased since 
New home and total vehicles sales (three-month moving average, YoY%)

  I NSTI TUTE

Florida’s metros have seen the largest decline when compared to other major cities 
Examining different cities across the US also sheds light on insurance trends. Consumers in major California metros saw the 
largest increases in 2026, following near double digit growth in 2025, likely driven by widespread property damage from 
wildfires, according to BofA Global Research (Exhibit 7). Major coastal metros in the Northeast like Boston and New York also 
experienced sizable increases as well.  
Conversely, many cities throughout the Sun Belt like Houston, Nashville and Dallas saw flat or even declining insurance 
payments. BofA Global Research points to several reasons for these declines: part of the equation in this region is that the 
massive runup in the cost of property insurance and domestic migration to the region through 2024 has since eased or reversed 
course.   
Exhibit 7: California major metros have seen steady insurance payment growth, while Miami metros have seen declines 
Median insurance payments by core based statistical areas (September to August yearly, YoY%)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
In Florida, legislation that went into effect in 2023, eventually made its way into insurance prices in 2025, leading to widespread 
declines in premiums. The state also has a “lookback period” of three years, where insurance profits can be deemed too high and 
a rebate is issued to consumers. It’s possible this rebate is factoring into declines this year, as some carriers only allow rebates 
upon renewals.  
The Sunshine State was a notable standout for declines, with Orlando, Tampa and Jacksonville each down around 8% YoY in 
2026, followed closely by Miami with a 7% decrease. Additionally, Bank of America payments data suggests that Jacksonville, 
Miami and Orlando all had median insurance payments above the national level in 2023, though their payments were fell below 
that in 2026 (Exhibit 8).  
Major metros in Texas also saw easing insurance payments relative to 2023, while Seattle, San Jose, and Riverside, CA are 
among the major metros that went from below the national median to above in the three-year period ending in 2026.

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
The differences between the total and per household card spending growth rate (if discussed) can be explained by the following reasons:  
1. Overall total card spending growth is partially boosted by the growth in the number of active cardholders in our sample. This could be due to 
an increasing customer base or inactive customers using their cards more frequently. 
2. Per household card spending growth only looks at households that complete at least five transactions with Bank of America cards in the 
month. Per household spending growth isolates impacts from a changing sample size, which could be unrelated to underlying economic 
momentum, and potential spending volatility from less active users. 
3. Overall total card spending includes small business card spending while per household card spending does not. 
4. Differences due to using processing dates (total card spending) versus transaction date (per household card spending). 
5. Other differences including household formations due to young adults moving in and out of their parent’s houses during COVID. 
Any household consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level. Whenever median household savings and 
checking balances are quoted, the data is based on a fixed cohort of households that had a consumer deposit account (checking and/or savings 
account) for all months from January 2019 through the most current month of data shown.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards are excluded. Data 
regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
Insurance payments include renters, auto or home, but do not include insurance escrowed into mortgage payments or deducted from a 
paycheck.  
We consider a measure of services necessity spending that includes but is not limited to groceries, utilities, and gasoline. Discretionary services 
includes but is not limited to charitable donations, leisure travel, entertainment, and professional/consumer services. Discretionary retail 
includes but is not limited to general merchandise, miscellaneous, clothing, electronics and furniture. It excludes categories like groceries and 
gasoline. Holiday spending is defined as items in which spending in the November-December period is usually at least 20% of total annual 
spending on the category.  Value and premium grocers are determined judgmentally on a proprietary analysis of merchants by equity analysts. 
The “more discretionary” retail category includes furniture, electronics, building materials, online marketplaces, clothing retailers and 
miscellaneous stores; while “partly discretionary” retail contains auto parts and services, health and personal care retailers, general merchandise 
and “mostly necessity”  retail contains gas and groceries. 
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. 
Any reference to card spending per household on gasoline includes all purchases at gasoline stations and might include purchases of non-gas 
items. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

預期，美元轉強 。歐洲央行如期升息一 碼，
歐元持平 。日本銀行官員增田和幸表示應進
一步升息 ，確保通膨不高於政策目標 ，美國
財政部長施壓日本升息預期 ，日圓較主要貨
幣顯強，未來留意主要央行政策動向。

► ECB於9月10日把三項政策利率各上調 25個
基點，存款利率將於9月16日升至2.50%。央
行指出中東 衝突持續 製造通膨壓力，同時警
告增長下行風險 。ECB升息後，市場已訂價
明年4月升息超過75基點。
► 日 銀 下 周9 月17 至18 日 開 會， 委 員 高 田 創
(Hajime Takata)警告，在金融條件仍寬鬆 、
日圓偏弱及能源與食品成本上升下 ，若基礎
通膨加速 ，央行或須較 快升息；他認為現時
1%的政策利率仍然偏低 。不過，他亦表示通
膨未有急升，故毋須一次加50個基點行動。
► 全球面對能源供給衝擊推高 通膨，利率上升
影響收入和需求 。ECB已選擇先行抑制第二
輪通膨效應 ，代價是融資成本及成長股折現
壓力； 日銀 若 確 認 升息， 則進一 步支 持日
圓、推高日本公債 收益，加速日圓套利交易
平倉。日圓轉 強的情況下 ，我們預期銀行及
精選保險機構 、公用事業 、進口主導零售／
必需消費品、本土電信與服務業或能受惠。

投資焦點美國公債
► 美國財政部 9月10日執行10年期與20年期公
債回購計畫，並擴大回購規模從20億增至60
億美元 ，欲對缺乏 流動性的長天期舊債進行
流動性支持 ，減輕公債拋售 潮引發期限 溢價
走升問題 ，然實際回購規模僅 51.8億美元並
低於預期的 60億至100億美元令市場失望 。
財政部亦公布例行 10年與30年期公債標售結
果，宣布執行擴大回購操作後 ，從投標倍數
與得標 減預期利率 差距來看 ，反映長 天期公
債需求不淡 ，然川普承諾 共和黨若在期中選
舉中獲勝 ，將向全國成年 公民發錢，恐導致
未來公債 供給量續升 ，後續視 美國政府能否

維持財政紀律逐步恢復市場信心。
► 美國非農新增就業數明顯較前月改善 ，反映
就業市 場韌 性 ， 體現 實體 經濟表 現優 於 預
期，然短期 通膨難 回政策目標，預期聯準會
最快9月重啟升息 ，2年期公債利率水準已反
映升息空間 ，考量財政問題 與美國升 息預期
同在 ，投資美國公債宜慎 ，留意Fed官員態
度與經濟預估變化。
對等關稅宣布後，內需防禦股獲利下修幅度最少 美國經濟未見明顯疲態！為聯準會埋下升息伏筆財政部倍增長期公債回購不如預期，然標售需求回溫
美財政部倍增公債回購不如預期，保守看待公債配置

### Page 7

7
投資焦點
► 2026年第2季美國標普500企業財報數據顯示
獲利成長明顯高於債務增速 ，另可發現代表
獲利護城河的大型企業毛利率貼近 2022年以
來高位，淨利率亦創1998年以來新高 ，資本
支出年增至 25%歷史高位水準 ，另資本 支出
佔EBITDA比率升至 35%，預期美國大型企
業持續擴大資本 支出推動獲利 成長動能 ，同
時維持獲利高於資本開支的資本效率 ，避免
大額資本支出過度失控 ，造成未來債信體質
與償債能力惡化。
► 從今年1月至8月美國各天期投資級債表現來
看，可以發現中短天期債含息總報酬仍維持
正值，主因美國政府財政 問題與升息擔憂推
升殖利率曲線上行 。從短、中、長天期殖利
率/存續期間比率來看 ，美國短天期債具備利
率波動下最大抗震能力 ，而長天期承受 利率
上行耐受幅度相對僅 50個基本點 ，考量利率
風險可能持續至年底 ，債市投資人可配置中
短天期債券降擾動。
對等關稅宣布後，內需防禦股獲利下修幅度最少 美國中短天期投資級債年至今表現相對較穩美國大型企業淨利率創1998年來新高

美國大型企業債信仍強，中短天期投資級債為首選

### Page 8

8
股市
 全球主要國家債務攀升 ，利率創高添股市評價修正風險 ，加上科技股舉債投入資本支出 ，未來獲利
與變現能力備受關注 。美伊衝突加劇不確定性 ，短線可透過布局醫療與公用事業等防禦股 ，降低市
場震盪風險 ，科技及AI半導體等發展趨勢不變 ，獲利等基本面支持長線發展 ，採逢回分批布局 ，並
分散國家與產業配置。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率彈升有利銀行股 ，歐、日銀行股基本面佳 ，日本半導體股
分批布局較穩健，歐洲國防軍工仍具投資吸引力。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融、工業等產業機會
區域：日本銀行股、日本半導體股、歐洲
金融、國防
債市
 發債供給與聯準會升息預期 ，美國公債殖利率續升，應避免超長天期債券投資 ，預期年底前美國10
年期公債利率將於4.5%-5.0%震盪，可逢利率走升時 ，以中短天期債券進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體
質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引力者 ，包含金融、科技、通訊及公

用事業等產業。
 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
 聯準會9月升息預期持續，美元短線高檔震盪。
 日本銀行釋出鷹派訊號，9月升息可能性升高，有利日圓止貶回升。
日圓震盪偏升
商品
 中東衝突反覆 ，荷姆茲海峽短期恢復正常通行機率偏低 ，加上石油庫存探低 ，預期今年能源仍呈供
不應求態勢，油價維持偏高水位。
 美國財務壓力與去美元化之兩個長期結構性問題依然存在，央行買盤提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金區間操作，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 債務供給與升息預期擾動，基本面形塑中長期布局契機

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。
